# Review — 033-rule-surface-setting

## Summary

Five passes over 033's window since its reopen (diff-base 37c8219c, base-source reopen) to HEAD. The window holds 033's own commits (its not-installed citation state, 155dc13c, and its scenarios), the commits touching its directory while naming other specs or none, and the commits naming no spec that this window shares with 020's, which starts at the same commit: the dropped-hosts chore b1d319f1, the pin and lockfile bumps, and the workflow and installer changes. Five agents each read the nine loaded rule files whole.

Coverage: examined counts 34 of the 41 scope entries, each read whole. unread names seven:

- runtime/CHANGELOG.md, 436 KB: 0ce70cd0's, 155dc13c's and b1d319f1's hunks read;
- runtime/src/schema/primitives.rs, 459 KB: 155dc13c's diff and the items it touches read;
- five files that changed only by this run's fixes and joined the scope uncommitted, which the fix-round re-read reads: framework/reference/plan.md and its mirror, generators.yml, the owner-reviews-067 mutation list and 081's plan.md.

AGENTS.md is counted whole as a file; its 45 window commits' hunks were read by their added lines, cut at 260 characters. specs/020-code-review/spec.md was read whole and left the scope when the plan's stale Affected Files row for it was removed.

Fixed in the run, both SHOULD findings:

- QUAL-TEST-001: an_installed_shipped_rule_is_found now installs a shipped rule and asserts it is found, with a second shipped rule not installed. Mutant M92, the shipped index deciding first, shows it failing.
- QUAL-CLAIM-001: gen-configure-mcp.sh's read loop keeps a last line with no trailing newline. test-gen-configure-mcp.sh's case F fails under the old loop.

Nine observations are fixed too:

- the out-of-surface test drops its inert config write;
- 033's plan states its runtime code and its Affected Files as built, and its cross-spec impact as it stands;
- spec.md cites §Rules (blocking and advisory) in framework/reference/analyze.md;
- the out-of-surface scenario's byte figure re-derives as 122,576 at ab2ad085;
- the generator's comment names the one configure file;
- the workflows' cache-key comment and 081's plan name the one include_str!, a correction that leaves 081 done;
- the lockfile takes five semver-compatible updates, committed alone as chore(022);
- the plan reference says each backticked path in a File cell joins review's scope;
- the analyze reference's host sentence moves to rule-file-unreadable.

Eight observations are waived by the rule, each with its reason. 033 stored no waivers or decisions, so process-waivers and process-decisions had nothing to apply.

1 fix round(s): 1 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.
